 return S;
}


/* =====================  PERSONNEL SCHEDULE (Phase 9)  =====================
   One schedule record = one inspection, one leave or one meeting. The people on it are ids of the
   existing user records (no second personnel table). Dates are 'YYYY-MM-DD' and times 'HH:MM', kept
   as text so the office time zone can never shift them. Conflict detection lives here so the server
   (which decides) and the browser (which displays) use exactly the same rules. */
const SCHED_TYPES=['inspection','leave','meeting'];
const leaveTypeById=id=>(S.leaveTypes||[]).find(t=>t.id===id)||null;
const leaveTypeName=id=>{const t=leaveTypeById(id);return t?t.name:'Leave';};
/* Everyone on a schedule: inspection = assigned personnel, leave = the one person, meeting = organizer + participants. */
function schedPeople(s){const a=[];if(s.type==='meeting'&&s.organizer)a.push(s.organizer);(s.personnel||[]).forEach(id=>{if(!a.includes(id))a.push(id);});return a;}
const hmMin=t=>{const a=String(t||'').split(':').map(Number);return a[0]*60+a[1];};
function fmtHM(t){if(!/^\d{2}:\d{2}$/.test(t||''))return '';const a=t.split(':').map(Number),h=a[0]%12||12;return h+':'+p2(a[1])+' '+(a[0]<12?'AM':'PM');}
const schedDates=s=>s.startDate===s.endDate?fmtD(parseD(s.startDate)):fmtD(parseD(s.startDate))+' – '+fmtD(parseD(s.endDate));
const schedTimes=s=>(s.type==='leave'||!s.startTime)?'Whole day':fmtHM(s.startTime)+' – '+fmtHM(s.endTime);
const schedTitle=s=>s.type==='leave'?leaveTypeName(s.leaveTypeId):(s.title||'');
/* Two schedules overlap when their dates meet and, if both have hours, the hours meet too.
   Leave is a whole-day matter, so any shared date with a leave is an overlap. */
function schedOverlap(a,b){
 if(a.startDate>b.endDate||b.startDate>a.endDate)return false;
 if(a.type==='leave'||b.type==='leave'||!a.startTime||!b.startTime)return true;
 return hmMin(a.startTime)<hmMin(b.endTime)&&hmMin(b.startTime)<hmMin(a.endTime);
}
/* Returns one entry per person per clashing schedule; an empty list means no conflict.
   'excludeId' is the schedule being edited, so it never conflicts with itself. */
function scheduleConflicts(c,excludeId){
 const out=[],mine=schedPeople(c);
 (S.schedules||[]).forEach(o=>{
  if(o.id===excludeId||(c.id&&o.id===c.id))return;
  const shared=schedPeople(o).filter(id=>mine.includes(id));
  if(!shared.length||!schedOverlap(c,o))return;
  const when=schedDates(o)+((o.type!=='leave'&&o.startTime)?', '+schedTimes(o):'');
  const detail=(o.type==='leave'?schedTitle(o):o.type[0].toUpperCase()+o.type.slice(1)+': '+schedTitle(o))+' – '+when;
  shared.forEach(id=>{
   const n=uname(id);let msg;
   if(c.type==='leave'&&o.type==='leave')msg=n+' already has '+schedTitle(o)+' that overlaps this leave.';
   else if(o.type==='leave')msg=n+' is already on '+schedTitle(o)+' during this scheduled '+c.type+'.';
   else if(c.type==='leave')msg=n+' is already scheduled for the '+o.type+' "'+schedTitle(o)+'" during this leave.';
   else msg=n+' is already scheduled for the '+o.type+' "'+schedTitle(o)+'" that overlaps this '+c.type+'.';
   out.push({userId:id,name:n,otherId:o.id,otherType:o.type,message:msg,detail:detail});
  });
 });
 return out;
}



/* =====================  SPECIAL REPORTS (Phase 11)  =====================
   Mandatory recurring reports the SYSTEM creates on a schedule (they are never logged by hand).
   A schedule (configuration) says which months apply, how many working days are allowed and who
   is responsible. Each due month produces ONE report obligation; each assignee gets an
   assignment that appears in For My Action.
   Deadlines reuse nthWorkdayOfMonth() (weekends and the holidays list are skipped) and the
   displayed status reuses dl(), so nothing here is a second deadline system.
   Months are stored as numbers 1-12. A period is identified by (schedule, year, month) where
   month is the "as of" month of the report (March for a quarter ending in March). */
const SR_FREQ=[['monthly','Monthly'],['quarterly','Quarterly'],['specific','Specific months'],['annual','Annual']];
/* deadline bases: only one today; the list exists so another basis can be added without touching callers */
const SR_BASES=[['working_days','Working days of the month after the reporting period']];
const SR_SPAN={monthly:1,specific:1,quarterly:3,annual:12};
const SR_HORIZON_MONTHS=24; /* missed generation is recovered for this many months back, never further */
const srFreqName=f=>(SR_FREQ.find(x=>x[0]===f)||[f,f])[1];
const isSrFreq=f=>SR_FREQ.some(x=>x[0]===f);
const isSrBasis=b=>SR_BASES.some(x=>x[0]===b);
const srCleanMonths=a=>[...new Set((Array.isArray(a)?a:[]).map(Number).filter(n=>Number.isInteger(n)&&n>=1&&n<=12))].sort((x,y)=>x-y);
function srMonthsLabel(months){
 const m=srCleanMonths(months);
 if(m.length===12)return 'January–December';
 return m.map(i=>MONTHS[i-1]).join(', ')||'—';
}
/* "January 2026", or "January–March 2026" for a quarter, "January–December 2026" for a year */
function srPeriodLabel(freq,month,year){
 const span=SR_SPAN[freq]||1;
 if(span===1)return MONTHS[month-1]+' '+year;
 const sm=month-span+1;
 if(sm>=1)return MONTHS[sm-1]+'–'+MONTHS[month-1]+' '+year;
 return MONTHS[sm+11]+' '+(year-1)+'–'+MONTHS[month-1]+' '+year;
}
const srTitle=(name,month,year)=>name+' as of '+MONTHS[month-1]+' '+year;
/* the month after the reporting month: where the report is generated and where its deadline is counted */
const srGenMonth=(month,year)=>({month:month===12?1:month+1,year:month===12?year+1:year});
const srGenerationDate=(month,year,day)=>{const g=srGenMonth(month,year),n=Math.max(1,Math.min(31,Number(day)||1));return new Date(g.year,g.month-1,Math.min(n,new Date(g.year,g.month,0).getDate()));};
function srWorkdaysIn(year,mi){let c=0;const last=new Date(year,mi+1,0).getDate();for(let d=1;d<=last;d++)if(isWork(new Date(year,mi,d)))c++;return c;}
/* The deadline: the n-th working day counted from the 1st of the month after the reporting month.
   When the threshold fits inside that month (15, 17, 20 working days in practice) this IS the n-th working day
   of that month, worked out by the existing nthWorkdayOfMonth(). When it does not fit (25 working days is more
   than any month has: February 2026 has 20) the count simply carries on into the next month, still skipping
   weekends and listed holidays, so the date is always a real working day (spills is set). */
function srDeadline(month,year,thr){
 const g=srGenMonth(month,year),mi=g.month-1,avail=srWorkdaysIn(g.year,mi),base=MONTHS[mi]+' '+g.year;
 if(thr<=avail){const due=nthWorkdayOfMonth(g.year,mi,thr);return{dueAt:isoD(due),genYear:g.year,genMonth:g.month,spills:false,label:'the '+ordinal(thr)+' working day of '+base};}
 let d=new Date(g.year,mi+1,1),left=thr-avail;
 while(!(isWork(d)&&--left<=0))d=addDays(d,1);
 return{dueAt:isoD(d),genYear:g.year,genMonth:g.month,spills:true,label:'the '+ordinal(thr)+' working day counted from the start of '+base+' (falls in '+MONTHS[d.getMonth()]+' '+d.getFullYear()+')'};
}
const srParsePeriod=v=>{const m=/^(\d{4})-(0[1-9]|1[0-2])$/.exec(String(v||''));return m?{year:Number(m[1]),month:Number(m[2])}:null;};
const srPeriodKey=(y,m)=>y+'-'+p2(m);
/* The reporting periods a schedule owes RIGHT NOW: every configured month, from the schedule's
   first period up to the latest one whose configured generation date in the following month has come.
   This is what makes a missed run recoverable: the answer depends only on the date, never on
   whether an earlier run happened. Existing reports are filtered out by the caller. */
function srDuePeriods(cfg,now){
 const today=sod(now||new Date()),months=srCleanMonths(cfg.months),from=srParsePeriod(cfg.fromPeriod);
 const out=[];if(!months.length||!from)return out;
 const floor=new Date(today.getFullYear(),today.getMonth()-SR_HORIZON_MONTHS,1);
 for(let y=from.year;y<=today.getFullYear();y++){
  for(const m of months){
   if(y===from.year&&m<from.month)continue;
   const g=srGenMonth(m,y),genDate=srGenerationDate(m,y,cfg.generationDay);
   if(genDate>today||genDate<floor)continue;
   out.push({year:y,month:m,genDate:isoD(genDate),genYear:g.year,genMonth:g.month});
  }
 }
 return out;
}
/* A whole schedule checked in one place; the server runs this again before saving.
   ctx.activeUser(id) and ctx.division(code) come from the caller. Returns '' when valid. */
function srValidate(c,ctx){
 ctx=ctx||{};
 if(!String(c.name||'').trim())return 'Enter the Special Report name.';
 if(!c.frequency)return 'Choose the frequency.';
 if(!isSrFreq(c.frequency))return 'Choose a valid frequency.';
 const generationDay=Number(c.generationDay==null?1:c.generationDay);
 if(!Number.isInteger(generationDay)||generationDay<1||generationDay>31)return 'Choose a generation day from 1 to 31.';
 const months=srCleanMonths(c.months);
 if(!months.length)return 'Choose at least one reporting month.';
 if(c.frequency==='monthly'&&months.length!==12)return 'A monthly report is generated every month. Use “Specific months” for a chosen set of months.';
 if(c.frequency==='annual'&&months.length!==1)return 'An annual report has exactly one reporting month.';
 if(c.frequency==='quarterly'){
  if(months.length!==4||months.some(m=>(m-months[0])%3!==0))return 'A quarterly report needs four reporting months, three months apart (for example March, June, September, December).';
 }
 const thr=Number(c.threshold);
 if(c.threshold===''||c.threshold==null||!Number.isInteger(thr))return 'Enter the deadline threshold as a whole number of working days.';
 if(thr<1||thr>60)return 'The deadline threshold must be between 1 and 60 working days.';
 if(!isSrBasis(c.basis))return 'Choose a valid deadline basis.';
 const users=Array.isArray(c.assignUsers)?c.assignUsers:[],divs=Array.isArray(c.assignDivisions)?c.assignDivisions:[];
 if(!users.length&&!divs.length)return 'Choose who is responsible: at least one person or one division.';
 if(ctx.activeUser&&users.some(id=>!ctx.activeUser(id)))return 'Choose responsible personnel from the active users. The System Administrator account has no For My Action list, so it cannot be assigned.';
 if(ctx.division&&divs.some(d=>!ctx.division(d)))return 'Choose divisions from the existing division list.';
 if(c.fromPeriod&&!srParsePeriod(c.fromPeriod))return 'The first reporting period must be a month, for example 2026-01.';
 return '';
}
/* status shown for a generated report: the existing deadline states, plus Pending / Completed wording */
function srStatus(item){
 const x=dl(item);
 if(item.status==='completed')return{key:x.key,chip:'Completed',tone:x.key==='done_late'?'bad':'ok',label:x.label,days:x.label};
 if(x.key==='over')return{key:'over',chip:'Overdue',tone:'bad',label:x.label,days:x.label};
 if(x.key==='soon')return{key:'soon',chip:x.n===0?'Due today':'Due soon',tone:'warn',label:x.label,days:x.label};
 return{key:x.key,chip:'Pending',tone:'info',label:x.label,days:x.label};
}
const srActiveAssign=item=>(item.assign||[]).filter(a=>a.status==='active');
const srIsMine=(item,userId)=>{
 if(item.status!=='pending')return false;
 const u=uById(userId),w=item.workflow;
 if(!u)return false;
 if(!w)return srActiveAssign(item).some(a=>a.userId===userId);
 const d=Object.assign({},item,w,{status:w.status||'processing'});
 if(d.status==='processing'&&!d.assignee&&srActiveAssign(item).some(a=>a.userId===userId))d.assignee=userId;
 return isMyTurn(u,d);
};

var ENGINE={act,logDoc,baseState,seedDemoDocs,migrateState,canView,has,can,isMyTurn,rebuildHS,uById,createDoc,getTx,isRegularDoc,audit,computeDeadline,computeDue,hasPrescribedDeadline,reportingPeriodInfo,normMonth,nthWorkdayOfMonth,firstWorkdayOfMonth,isWork,addWD,wdBetween,isoD,parseD,docTypeOf,docTypeBasis,docTypeUnit,docTypeThreshold,isDeadlineBasis,activeDocTypes,activeCommTypes,commName,DEADLINE_UNITS,DEADLINE_BASES,MONTHS,REGULAR_DOC,REGULAR_DAYS,PERMS,ROLES,ROLE_ORDER,DEFAULT_PERMS,DEFAULT_PW,ADMIN_PW,isDivFocal,deptApprovers,REVIEW_LINE,pwPolicy:null,SCHED_TYPES,schedPeople,schedOverlap,scheduleConflicts,leaveTypeName,defaultLeaveTypes,SR_FREQ,SR_BASES,SR_SPAN,srFreqName,isSrFreq,isSrBasis,srCleanMonths,srMonthsLabel,srPeriodLabel,srTitle,srGenMonth,srGenerationDate,srDeadline,srParsePeriod,srPeriodKey,srDuePeriods,srValidate,srStatus,srActiveAssign,srIsMine,dl};

FILE: public/workflow.js

'use strict';
const WF={rows:[],modes:[],versions:[],current:null,errors:[],sim:null,zoom:1,loaded:false};
const wfTypes=['START','REVIEW','APPROVAL','DECISION','RETURN','ASSIGNMENT','NOTIFICATION','CLEAR_FOR_OUT','END'];
const wfUid=()=>crypto.randomUUID();
function wfIcon(type){const icons={START:'<circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10"/><path d="m10 7 7 5-7 5z" fill="currentColor" stroke="none"/>',REVIEW:'<rect x="2" y="2" width="20" height="20" rx="5"/><circle cx="12" cy="8" r="3"/><path d="M6 19c.6-3 2.5-4.5 6-4.5s5.4 1.5 6 4.5"/>',APPROVAL:'<rect x="2" y="2" width="20" height="20" rx="5"/><path d="m6 12 4 4 8-9"/>',DECISION:'<path d="M12 1 23 12 12 23 1 12z"/><path d="M12 6v12M7 10l5-4 5 4M7 14l5 4 5-4"/>',RETURN:'<rect x="2" y="2" width="20" height="20" rx="5"/><path d="M9 8 5 12l4 4M5 12h8a5 5 0 0 1 5 5"/>',ASSIGNMENT:'<rect x="2" y="2" width="20" height="20" rx="5"/><rect x="9" y="5" width="6" height="5" rx="1"/><rect x="4" y="15" width="6" height="5" rx="1"/><rect x="14" y="15" width="6" height="5" rx="1"/><path d="M12 10v2.5H7v2.5m5-2.5h5v2.5"/>',NOTIFICATION:'<rect x="2" y="2" width="20" height="20" rx="5"/><path d="M7 16h10l-1.5-2.5V10a3.5 3.5 0 0 0-7 0v3.5zM10 19h4"/>',CLEAR_FOR_OUT:'<rect x="2" y="2" width="20" height="20" rx="5"/><path d="M7 20V4m1 1h9l-2 3 2 3H8"/><path d="m12 14 2 2 4-4"/>',END:'<rect x="2" y="2" width="20" height="20" rx="5"/><rect x="8" y="8" width="8" height="8" rx="1" fill="currentColor" stroke="none"/>'};return '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true">'+icons[type]+'</svg>';}
function wfGraph(){const labels=['Document received','Admin Staff review','Focal / Supervisor review','Staff processing','Supervisor review','Division Manager review','Department Manager / acting approver','Clear for Out','Released / Out','Return for revision'];const types=['START','REVIEW','REVIEW','ASSIGNMENT','REVIEW','REVIEW','REVIEW','CLEAR_FOR_OUT','END','RETURN'];const roles=['','admin_staff','focal','','supervisor','div_manager','dept_manager','','',''];const nodes=labels.map((label,i)=>({id:'n'+i,type:types[i],label,x:30+i%5*240,y:80+Math.floor(i/5)*230,config:roles[i]?{responsibilityType:'ROLE',role:roles[i]}:(i===3||i===9?{responsibilityType:'CURRENT_ASSIGNEE'}:{})}));const pairs=[[0,1,'Received'],[1,2,'Forward'],[2,3,'Assign'],[3,4,'Submit'],[4,5,'Approve'],[5,6,'Approve'],[6,7,'Clear'],[7,8,'Log out'],[4,9,'Return'],[9,3,'Revise']];return{nodes,edges:pairs.map((p,i)=>({id:'e'+i,source:'n'+p[0],target:'n'+p[1],label:p[2],condition:{}}))};}
const wfRegistry=Object.fromEntries(wfTypes.map(type=>[type,{type,label:({START:'Start',END:'End',CLEAR_FOR_OUT:'Clear for Out',RETURN:'Return / Revision'})[type]||type[0]+type.slice(1).toLowerCase(),category:type==='START'?'Triggers':['REVIEW','APPROVAL'].includes(type)?'Approvals':type==='DECISION'?'Conditions':['RETURN','CLEAR_FOR_OUT','END'].includes(type)?'Document':'Actions',outputs:type==='END'?[]:type==='DECISION'?['TRUE','FALSE']:type==='APPROVAL'?['APPROVED','REJECTED','RETURNED']:['next'],input:type!=='START',responsible:['REVIEW','APPROVAL','ASSIGNMENT'].includes(type)}]));
const wfActionTypes=['REVIEW','APPROVAL','ASSIGNMENT','NOTIFICATION','RETURN','CLEAR_FOR_OUT'];
wfRegistry.ACTION={type:'ACTION',label:'Action',category:'Actions',outputs:['next'],input:true,responsible:true};
Object.assign(WF,{selectedIds:[],selectedEdge:null,undo:[],redo:[],clipboard:null,dirty:false,saving:false,saveError:'',search:'',snap:true,minimap:true,space:false,loading:false,revision:0,layoutDirection:'LR',layoutMode:'hierarchical',diagramTheme:'pastel',hand:false});
const wfEditable=()=>WF.current&&WF.current.status==='DRAFT';
const wfGraphState=()=>JSON.stringify({nodes:WF.current.nodes,edges:WF.current.edges});
function wfCheckpoint(){if(!wfEditable())return;WF.undo.push(wfGraphState());if(WF.undo.length>60)WF.undo.shift();WF.redo=[];}
function wfChanged(){WF.dirty=true;WF.revision++;WF.errors=[];WF.sim=null;wfDrawStatus();}
function wfDrawStatus(){const el=document.querySelector('#wfSaveStatus');if(el)el.textContent=WF.saving?'Saving…':WF.saveError?'Save failed: '+WF.saveError:WF.dirty?'Unsaved changes':'Saved';}
function wfDraw(){const el=document.querySelector('.wf-editor');if(el){const board=el.querySelector('#wfBoard'),pos=board?{x:board.scrollLeft,y:board.scrollTop}:null;el.outerHTML=wfEditor();const next=document.querySelector('#wfBoard');if(next&&pos){next.scrollLeft=pos.x;next.scrollTop=pos.y;}}else render();}
function wfReset(w){WF.current=w;WF.selectedIds=[];WF.selectedEdge=null;WF.undo=[];WF.redo=[];WF.dirty=false;WF.saveError='';WF.errors=w.validation||[];WF.sim=null;WF.simError='';WF.connect=null;}
async function wfList(){const r=await api('GET','/api/workflows');if(r.ok){WF.rows=r.workflows||[];WF.modes=r.modes||[];WF.loaded=true;}else toast(r.error||'Could not load workflows.','bad');return r.ok;}
async function wfOpen(id,version){if(WF.dirty){toast('Save this draft before switching workflows.','bad');return;}const meta=await api('GET','/api/workflows/'+id);if(!meta.ok)return toast(meta.error,'bad');WF.versions=meta.versions||[];const v=version||WF.versions[0]?.id;if(!v)return;const r=await api('GET','/api/workflows/'+id+'/versions/'+v);if(!r.ok)return toast(r.error,'bad');wfReset(r.workflow);render();}
function wfCreateDialog(convert){openModal(convert?'Convert existing workflow':'Create workflow','<div data-form="wfcreate"><div class="fld"><label>Workflow name</label><input name="name" value="'+(convert?'Existing document workflow':'')+'" maxlength="120"></div><div class="fld"><label>Document type</label><select name="docType">'+S.refs.doc.map(x=>opt(x.name,x.name)).join('')+'</select></div><p class="hint">Creates a draft. Publishing and activation are separate.</p></div>',{foot:'<button class="btn" data-act="closeModal">Cancel</button><button class="btn primary" data-act="wfCreate" data-convert="'+(convert?'1':'0')+'">Create draft</button>'});}
async function wfCreate(convert){if(WF.dirty)return toast('Save this draft before creating another workflow.','bad');const v=formVals(document.querySelector('[data-form="wfcreate"]'));if(!v.name||!v.docType)return toast('Name and document type are required.','bad');const r=await api('POST','/api/workflows',{name:v.name,docType:v.docType,definition:convert?wfGraph():{nodes:[],edges:[]}});if(!r.ok)return toast(r.error,'bad');closeModal();wfReset(r.workflow);WF.versions=[{id:r.workflow.versionId,version:1,status:'DRAFT'}];await wfList();render();}
async function wfSave(){if(!WF.current)return false;if(!wfEditable())return true;if(WF.saving)return false;WF.saving=true;WF.saveError='';wfDrawStatus();const w=WF.current,revision=WF.revision;try{const r=await api('PUT','/api/workflows/'+w.id+'/versions/'+w.versionId,{name:w.name,docType:w.docType,expectedRevision:w.revision,definition:JSON.parse(wfGraphState())});if(!r.ok){WF.saveError=r.error||'Could not save draft.';return false;}w.revision=r.workflow.revision;if(WF.revision===revision){WF.dirty=false;}return true;}finally{WF.saving=false;wfDrawStatus();}}
function wfSize(n){return {width:190,height:n.type==='DECISION'?160:['START','END'].includes(n.type)?64:120};}
function wfNodeContent(n){const def=wfRegistry[n.type];return '<span class="wf-palette-icon wf-icon-'+n.type.toLowerCase()+'">'+wfIcon(n.type==='ACTION'?'ASSIGNMENT':n.type)+'</span><span class="wf-node-copy">'+(n.label&&n.label!==def.label?'<small>'+esc(def.label)+'</small>':'')+'<b>'+esc(n.label||def.label)+'</b>'+((n.config||{}).description?'<span class="wf-node-description">'+esc(n.config.description)+'</span>':'')+'</span>';}
function wfNode(n,palette){const def=wfRegistry[n.type];if(palette)return '<div class="wf-palette-node wf-node-shell wf-shape-'+n.type.toLowerCase()+'" draggable="'+!!wfEditable()+'" data-palette="'+n.type+'" title="Drag onto the canvas">'+wfNodeContent(n)+'</div>';const outs=def.outputs,selected=WF.selectedIds.includes(n.id);return '<article class="wf-node wf-node-shell wf-shape-'+n.type.toLowerCase()+(selected?' selected':'')+((n.config||{}).enabled===false?' wf-disabled':'')+'" data-wfn="'+esc(n.id)+'" style="left:'+n.x+'px;top:'+n.y+'px;height:'+wfSize(n).height+'px">'+(def.input?'<button class="wf-handle wf-input" data-input="'+esc(n.id)+'" data-side="left" title="Input" aria-label="Input"></button><button class="wf-handle wf-input wf-input-right" data-input="'+esc(n.id)+'" data-side="right" title="Return input (right side)" aria-label="Return input"></button>':'')+'<div class="wf-node-body">'+wfNodeContent(n)+'</div>'+outs.map((out,i)=>'<button class="wf-handle wf-output" style="top:'+((i+1)/(outs.length+1)*100)+'%" data-output="'+esc(n.id)+'" data-handle="'+out+'" title="Drag '+out+' to another node’s input" aria-label="'+out+' output"></button>').join('')+'</article>';}
function wfRoute(x1,y1,x2,y2){const m=x2>=x1+40?(x1+x2)/2:Math.max(x1,x2)+40;return 'M'+x1+' '+y1+' H'+m+' V'+y2+' H'+x2;}
function wfEdgeGeometry(e){const a=WF.current.nodes.find(n=>n.id===e.source),b=WF.current.nodes.find(n=>n.id===e.target);if(!a||!b)return null;const outs=wfRegistry[a.type].outputs,index=Math.max(0,outs.indexOf((e.condition||{}).sourceHandle||'next')),x1=a.x+190,y1=a.y+wfSize(a).height*(index+1)/(outs.length+1);const side=e.condition?.targetSide||'auto',returning=side==='right'||(side==='auto'&&(b.x<a.x||a.type==='RETURN'||/return|revise|comments/i.test(e.label||'')));if(returning){const x2=b.x+190,y2=b.y+wfSize(b).height*.9,offset=48+WF.current.edges.indexOf(e)*12,bottom=Math.max(a.y+wfSize(a).height,b.y+wfSize(b).height)+offset;return{d:'M'+x1+' '+y1+' H'+(x1+28)+' V'+bottom+' H'+(x2+28)+' V'+y2+' H'+x2,x:(x1+x2)/2+28,y:bottom-8};}const x2=b.x,y2=b.y+wfSize(b).height/2,m=x2>=x1+40?(x1+x2)/2:Math.max(x1,x2)+40;return{d:wfRoute(x1,y1,x2,y2),x:(x1+m)/2,y:y1-8};}

function wfLines(){return '<defs><marker id="wfa" markerWidth="8" markerHeight="8" refX="6" refY="3" orient="auto"><path d="M0 0 L0 6 L7 3z" fill="currentColor"/></marker></defs>'+WF.current.edges.map(e=>{const g=wfEdgeGeometry(e);return g?'<g data-edge="'+esc(e.id)+'" class="wf-edge '+(WF.selectedEdge===e.id?'selected':'')+'"><path class="wf-edge-hit" d="'+g.d+'"/><path class="wf-edge-line" d="'+g.d+'" marker-end="url(#wfa)"/><text x="'+g.x+'" y="'+g.y+'">'+esc(e.label||'')+'</text></g>':'';}).join('');}
function wfUpdateLines(){const svg=document.querySelector('.wf-lines');if(svg)svg.innerHTML=wfLines();wfUpdateMini();}
function wfField(label,key,value,options){return '<div class="fld"><label>'+label+'</label>'+(options?'<select data-wf-field="'+key+'">'+options.map(o=>opt(o[0],o[1],String(value||'')===String(o[0]))).join('')+'</select>':['config.description','config.notes'].includes(key)?'<textarea rows="4" data-wf-field="'+key+'">'+esc(value??'')+'</textarea>':'<input data-wf-field="'+key+'" value="'+esc(value??'')+'">')+'</div>';}
function wfProperties(){const edge=WF.current.edges.find(e=>e.id===WF.selectedEdge),node=WF.current.nodes.find(n=>n.id===WF.selectedIds[0]);if(edge){const c=edge.condition||{},by=Object.fromEntries(WF.current.nodes.map(n=>[n.id,n]));return '<b>Connection properties</b><p>'+esc(by[edge.source]?.label||'?')+' → '+esc(by[edge.target]?.label||'?')+'</p><p class="hint">Output: '+esc(c.sourceHandle||'next')+' · Input: in</p>'+wfField('Label','edge.label',edge.label)+wfField('Target connection side','edge.targetSide',c.targetSide||'auto',[['auto','Automatic (returns enter right)'],['left','Left'],['right','Right / return']])+'<p class="hint">Received · Forward · Assign · Submit · Approve · Clear · Log out · Return · TRUE · FALSE</p>'+wfField('Condition field','edge.field',c.field,[['','Always'],['status','Status'],['priority','Priority'],['docType','Document type'],['division','Division']])+wfField('Operator','edge.operator',c.operator||'equals',[['equals','Equals'],['not_equals','Does not equal'],['contains','Contains']])+wfField('Value','edge.value',c.value||'')+'<button class="btn danger" data-wf-action="delete">Delete connection</button>';}
if(!node)return '<b>Properties</b><p class="hint">Drag a node from the palette. Drag an output handle onto an input handle to connect. Select a node or connection to edit.</p>';const cfg=node.config||{},def=wfRegistry[node.type];return '<b>Node properties</b><div class="row"><button class="btn sm" data-wf-action="duplicate">Duplicate</button><button class="btn sm danger" data-wf-action="delete">Delete</button></div>'+wfField(wfActionTypes.includes(node.type)?'Action type':'Node type','type',node.type,(wfActionTypes.includes(node.type)?wfActionTypes:['START','DECISION','END',...wfActionTypes]).map(t=>[t,wfRegistry[t].label]))+wfField('Name','label',node.label)+wfField('Description','config.description',cfg.description||'')+wfField('Notes','config.notes',cfg.notes||'')+(def.responsible?wfField('Responsibility','config.responsibilityType',cfg.responsibilityType||'ROLE',[['ROLE','Role'],['USER','Specific user'],['DIVISION','Division'],['ROLE_DIVISION','Role + division'],['CURRENT_ASSIGNEE','Current assignee']])+(['ROLE','ROLE_DIVISION'].includes(cfg.responsibilityType||'ROLE')?wfField('Role','config.role',cfg.role||'admin_staff',Object.entries(ROLES).filter(x=>x[0]!=='sysadmin')):'')+(cfg.responsibilityType==='USER'?wfField('User','config.userId',cfg.userId,[['','Choose user'],...S.users.filter(u=>u.active).map(u=>[u.id,u.name])]):'')+(['DIVISION','ROLE_DIVISION'].includes(cfg.responsibilityType)?wfField('Division','config.division',cfg.division,[['','Choose division'],...S.divisions.map(d=>[d.code,d.code])]):''):'');}
function wfMini(){if(!WF.minimap)return '';return '<svg class="wf-minimap" viewBox="0 0 2400 1200" aria-label="Workflow minimap">'+WF.current.edges.map(e=>{const g=wfEdgeGeometry(e);return g?'<path d="'+g.d+'"/>':''}).join('')+WF.current.nodes.map(n=>'<rect x="'+n.x+'" y="'+n.y+'" width="190" height="'+wfSize(n).height+'"/>').join('')+'<rect class="wf-mini-viewport" x="0" y="0" width="600" height="400"/></svg>';}
function wfUpdateMini(){const old=document.querySelector('.wf-minimap');if(old)old.outerHTML=wfMini();const board=document.querySelector('#wfBoard'),rect=document.querySelector('.wf-mini-viewport');if(board&&rect){rect.setAttribute('x',board.scrollLeft/WF.zoom);rect.setAttribute('y',board.scrollTop/WF.zoom);rect.setAttribute('width',board.clientWidth/WF.zoom);rect.setAttribute('height',board.clientHeight/WF.zoom);}}
function wfPalette(){const paletteDefs=['START','ACTION','DECISION','END'].map(t=>wfRegistry[t]);const groups=[...new Set(paletteDefs.map(d=>d.category))];return '<b>Node palette</b><input id="wfSearch" placeholder="Search nodes…" value="'+esc(WF.search)+'">'+groups.map(group=>'<details open><summary>'+group+'</summary>'+paletteDefs.filter(d=>d.category===group&&(d.label+' '+d.type).toLowerCase().includes(WF.search.toLowerCase())).map(d=>wfNode({type:d.type,label:d.label},true)).join('')+'</details>').join('');}
function wfEditor(){const w=WF.current;if(!w)return '<div class="card pad"><h2>Build your workflow</h2><p>Select an existing workflow or create a draft to start designing.</p></div>';const button=(action,label)=>'<button class="btn sm" data-wf-action="'+action+'">'+label+'</button>';return '<section class="wf-editor" data-diagram-theme="'+WF.diagramTheme+'"><header class="wf-toolbar"><div><b>'+esc(w.name)+'</b> '+badge(w.status==='PUBLISHED'?'ok':'warn',w.status+' · v'+w.version)+'<div id="wfSaveStatus" class="hint">'+(WF.dirty?'Unsaved changes':'Saved')+'</div></div><div class="row">'+button('save','Save')+(wfEditable()?button('discard','Discard changes'):'')+button('undo','Undo')+button('redo','Redo')+button('validate','Validate')+button('simulate','Simulate')+'<select id="wfTheme" aria-label="Diagram theme">'+[['pastel','Pastel theme'],['slate','Slate theme'],['minimal','Minimal theme']].map(o=>opt(o[0],o[1],WF.diagramTheme===o[0])).join('')+'</select><select id="wfLayoutMode" aria-label="Layout algorithm">'+[['hierarchical','Hierarchical'],['adaptive','Adaptive']].map(o=>opt(o[0],o[1],WF.layoutMode===o[0])).join('')+'</select><select id="wfDirection" aria-label="Layout direction">'+[['LR','Left to right'],['RL','Right to left'],['TB','Top to bottom'],['BT','Bottom to top']].map(o=>opt(o[0],o[1],WF.layoutDirection===o[0])).join('')+'</select>'+button('layout','Arrange')+button('fit','Fit view')+button('grid',WF.snap?'Grid on':'Grid off')+button('minimap','Minimap')+(wfEditable()?button('publish','Publish'):button('fork','New draft'))+'</div></header><div class="wf-layout"><aside class="wf-palette">'+wfPalette()+'</aside><div class="wf-board" id="wfBoard"><div class="wf-scene" style="width:'+Math.max(2400,...w.nodes.map(n=>n.x+400))+'px;height:'+Math.max(1200,...w.nodes.map(n=>n.y+240))+'px;transform:scale('+WF.zoom+')"><svg class="wf-lines" style="width:100%;height:100%">'+wfLines()+'</svg>'+w.nodes.map(n=>wfNode(n,false)).join('')+(!w.nodes.length?'<div class="wf-empty"><b>Build your workflow</b><p>Drag a node from the palette onto the canvas.</p></div>':'')+'</div><div class="wf-canvas-controls">'+button('hand',WF.hand?'Hand on':'Pan')+button('zoomout','−')+'<span id="wfZoomLabel">'+Math.round(WF.zoom*100)+'%</span>'+button('zoomin','＋')+button('resetzoom','100%')+'</div>'+wfMini()+'</div><aside class="wf-properties"><fieldset '+(!wfEditable()?'disabled':'')+'>'+wfProperties()+'</fieldset></aside></div>'+(WF.errors.length?'<div class="banner bad"><b>Validation issues</b><ul>'+WF.errors.map(e=>'<li>'+esc(e)+'</li>').join('')+'</ul></div>':'')+(WF.simError?'<div class="banner bad">'+esc(WF.simError)+'</div>':'')+'<div class="card pad"><label>Simulation document</label><select id="wfSimDoc"><option value="">Synthetic document</option>'+S.docs.map(d=>opt(d.id,d.trackNo+' · '+d.subject,WF.simDoc===d.id)).join('')+'</select><p class="hint">Conditions use this document. Simulation never changes its status or history.</p>'+w.nodes.filter(n=>w.edges.filter(e=>e.source===n.id).length>1).map(n=>'<label>'+esc(n.label)+' outcome</label><select data-wf-branch="'+esc(n.id)+'"><option value="">First matching branch</option>'+w.edges.filter(e=>e.source===n.id).map(e=>opt(e.label,e.label||'Unlabeled branch',WF.branches?.[n.id]===e.label)).join('')+'</select>').join('')+'</div>'+(WF.sim?'<div class="card pad"><h3>Simulation</h3><p>'+esc(WF.sim.terminated?'Reached a terminal node.':'WARNING: did not reach an end node.')+'</p><ol>'+WF.sim.path.map(n=>'<li>'+esc(n.label)+' <small>'+esc((n.responsibleUsers||[]).map(u=>u.name).join(', ')||n.responsibility?.role||'')+'</small></li>').join('')+'</ol>'+((WF.sim.warnings||[]).map(x=>'<p class="hint">'+esc(x)+'</p>').join(''))+'</div>':'')+'</section>';}
VIEWS.workflow=function(u){if(!u||u.role!=='sysadmin')return '<div class="wrap">Workflow administration requires a System Administrator.</div>';if(!WF.loaded&&!WF.loading){WF.loading=true;wfList().finally(()=>{WF.loading=false;if(UI.route==='workflow')render();});}return '<div class="wrap wf-full"><div class="pagehead"><div><h1>Workflow Designer</h1><p>Build and configure workflow drafts.</p></div><div class="row"><select id="wfSwitch" aria-label="Choose workflow"><option value="">Choose workflow…</option>'+WF.rows.map(w=>opt(w.id,w.name+' · '+w.doc_type,WF.current?.id===w.id)).join('')+'</select>'+(WF.current?'<select id="wfVersion" aria-label="Choose version">'+WF.versions.map(v=>opt(v.id,'v'+v.version+' · '+v.status,v.id===WF.current.versionId)).join('')+'</select>':'')+'<button class="btn" data-wf-action="new">Create workflow</button><button class="btn" data-wf-action="convert">Convert existing workflow</button></div></div>'+wfEditor()+'</div>';};
function wfUndo(redo){if(!wfEditable())return;const from=redo?WF.redo:WF.undo,to=redo?WF.undo:WF.redo;if(!from.length)return;to.push(wfGraphState());const graph=JSON.parse(from.pop());Object.assign(WF.current,graph);WF.selectedIds=[];WF.selectedEdge=null;wfChanged();wfDraw();}
function wfDelete(){if(!wfEditable())return;wfCheckpoint();const ids=new Set(WF.selectedIds);WF.current.nodes=WF.current.nodes.filter(n=>!ids.has(n.id));WF.current.edges=WF.current.edges.filter(e=>e.id!==WF.selectedEdge&&!ids.has(e.source)&&!ids.has(e.target));WF.selectedIds=[];WF.selectedEdge=null;wfChanged();wfDraw();}
function wfCopy(){WF.clipboard=JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(WF.current.nodes.filter(n=>WF.selectedIds.includes(n.id))));}
function wfPaste(){if(!wfEditable()||!WF.clipboard?.length)return;wfCheckpoint();const nodes=WF.clipboard.map(n=>({...JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(n)),id:wfUid(),x:n.x+32,y:n.y+32}));WF.current.nodes.push(...nodes);WF.selectedIds=nodes.map(n=>n.id);wfChanged();wfDraw();}
function wfFit(){const board=document.querySelector('#wfBoard');if(!board||!WF.current.nodes.length)return;const ns=WF.current.nodes,minX=Math.min(...ns.map(n=>n.x)),minY=Math.min(...ns.map(n=>n.y)),maxX=Math.max(...ns.map(n=>n.x+190)),maxY=Math.max(...ns.map(n=>n.y+wfSize(n).height));WF.zoom=Math.max(.15,Math.min(1.5,(board.clientWidth-70)/(maxX-minX+70),(board.clientHeight-70)/(maxY-minY+70)));wfDraw();const b=document.querySelector('#wfBoard');b.scrollLeft=Math.max(0,minX*WF.zoom-35);b.scrollTop=Math.max(0,minY*WF.zoom-35);wfUpdateMini();}
function wfLayout(){if(!wfEditable())return;wfCheckpoint();const levels=new Map(),start=WF.current.nodes.find(n=>n.type==='START'),queue=start?[start.id]:[];if(start)levels.set(start.id,0);while(queue.length){const id=queue.shift();WF.current.edges.filter(e=>e.source===id).forEach(e=>{if(!levels.has(e.target)){levels.set(e.target,levels.get(id)+1);queue.push(e.target);}});}const groups=new Map();WF.current.nodes.forEach(n=>{const level=levels.get(n.id)??0;if(!groups.has(level))groups.set(level,[]);groups.get(level).push(n);});const maxLevel=Math.max(0,...groups.keys());for(const [level,nodes] of [...groups.entries()].sort((a,b)=>a[0]-b[0])){if(WF.layoutMode==='adaptive'){const score=n=>{const predecessors=WF.current.edges.filter(e=>e.target===n.id).map(e=>WF.current.nodes.find(x=>x.id===e.source)).filter(Boolean);return predecessors.length?predecessors.reduce((sum,p)=>sum+(['LR','RL'].includes(WF.layoutDirection)?p.y:p.x),0)/predecessors.length:0;};nodes.sort((a,b)=>score(a)-score(b));}nodes.forEach((n,index)=>{const reverse=['RL','BT'].includes(WF.layoutDirection),rank=reverse?maxLevel-level:level;if(['LR','RL'].includes(WF.layoutDirection)){n.x=80+rank*300;n.y=80+index*220;}else{n.x=80+index*280;n.y=80+rank*240;}});}wfChanged();wfDraw();wfFit();}

async function wfAction(a){if(a==='new')return wfCreateDialog(false);if(a==='convert')return wfCreateDialog(true);if(!WF.current)return;if(a==='hand'){WF.hand=!WF.hand;wfDraw();return;}if(a==='discard'){if(!wfEditable()||WF.saving)return;openModal('Discard unsaved changes?','<p>Reload the last saved draft. Unsaved nodes, connections, and property edits will be discarded.</p>',{foot:'<button class="btn" data-act="closeModal">Keep editing</button><button class="btn danger" data-wf-action="confirmDiscard">Discard changes</button>'});return;}if(a==='confirmDiscard'){const w=WF.current,r=await api('GET','/api/workflows/'+w.id+'/versions/'+w.versionId);if(!r.ok)return toast(r.error||'Could not reload saved draft.','bad');closeModal();wfReset(r.workflow);render();toast('Saved draft restored.');return;}if(a==='save'){if(await wfSave())toast('Draft saved.');else toast(WF.saveError||'Save in progress.','bad');return;}if(a==='undo')return wfUndo(false);if(a==='redo')return wfUndo(true);if(a==='delete')return wfDelete();if(a==='copy')return wfCopy();if(a==='paste')return wfPaste();if(a==='duplicate'){wfCopy();return wfPaste();}if(a==='fit')return wfFit();if(a==='layout')return wfLayout();if(a==='grid'){WF.snap=!WF.snap;return wfDraw();}if(a==='minimap'){WF.minimap=!WF.minimap;return wfDraw();}if(['zoomin','zoomout','resetzoom'].includes(a)){WF.zoom=a==='resetzoom'?1:Math.max(.2,Math.min(2,WF.zoom+(a==='zoomin'?.1:-.1)));wfDraw();return;}if(a==='fork'){const r=await api('POST','/api/workflows/'+WF.current.id+'/versions/'+WF.current.versionId+'/fork',{});if(r.ok){await wfList();await wfOpen(WF.current.id,r.workflow.versionId);}else toast(r.error,'bad');return;}
if(['validate','publish','simulate'].includes(a)){if(!await wfSave())return toast(WF.saveError||'Save in progress.','bad');const w=WF.current,url='/api/workflows/'+w.id+'/versions/'+w.versionId;if(a==='simulate'){WF.simError='';const r=await api('POST',url+'/simulate',{documentId:WF.simDoc||'',branchLabels:WF.branches||{}});if(r.ok)WF.sim=r;else WF.simError=r.error||'Simulation failed.';wfDraw();return;}const result=await api('POST',url+'/validate',{});if(!result.ok)return toast(result.error,'bad');WF.errors=result.errors||[];wfDraw();if(a==='validate')return toast(WF.errors.length?'Fix the listed validation issues.':'Workflow is valid.',WF.errors.length?'bad':'');if(WF.errors.length)return;openModal('Publish '+w.name+' v'+w.version,'<p>This valid version will become immutable and available for later activation.</p>',{foot:'<button class="btn" data-act="closeModal">Cancel</button><button class="btn primary" data-wf-action="confirmPublish">Publish version</button>'});}if(a==='confirmPublish'){const w=WF.current,r=await api('POST','/api/workflows/'+w.id+'/versions/'+w.versionId+'/publish',{});if(r.ok){closeModal();wfReset(r.workflow);await wfList();await wfOpen(w.id,r.workflow.versionId);}else{WF.errors=r.errors||[];toast(r.error,'bad');wfDraw();}}}
document.addEventListener('click',ev=>{if(UI.route!=='workflow')return;const action=ev.target.closest('[data-wf-action]');if(action){wfAction(action.dataset.wfAction);return;}const create=ev.target.closest('[data-act="wfCreate"]');if(create){wfCreate(create.dataset.convert==='1');return;}if(wfGesture?.kind==='move'&&wfGesture.moved)return;const node=ev.target.closest('[data-wfn]'),edge=ev.target.closest('[data-edge]');if(node&&!ev.target.closest('.wf-handle')){const id=node.dataset.wfn;WF.selectedIds=ev.ctrlKey||ev.shiftKey?(WF.selectedIds.includes(id)?WF.selectedIds.filter(x=>x!==id):[...WF.selectedIds,id]):[id];WF.selectedEdge=null;wfDraw();}else if(edge){WF.selectedEdge=edge.dataset.edge;WF.selectedIds=[];wfDraw();}else if(ev.target.closest('#wfBoard')&&!ev.target.closest('button')){WF.selectedIds=[];WF.selectedEdge=null;wfDraw();}});
document.addEventListener('change',ev=>{if(UI.route!=='workflow')return;if(ev.target.id==='wfDirection'){WF.layoutDirection=ev.target.value;return;}if(ev.target.id==='wfLayoutMode'){WF.layoutMode=ev.target.value;return;}if(ev.target.id==='wfTheme'){WF.diagramTheme=ev.target.value;wfDraw();return;}if(ev.target.dataset.wfBranch){WF.branches=WF.branches||{};WF.branches[ev.target.dataset.wfBranch]=ev.target.value;}if(ev.target.id==='wfSimDoc')WF.simDoc=ev.target.value;if(ev.target.id==='wfSwitch'&&ev.target.value)wfOpen(ev.target.value);if(ev.target.id==='wfVersion')wfOpen(WF.current.id,ev.target.value);if(ev.target.matches('[data-wf-field]')&&!['INPUT','TEXTAREA'].includes(ev.target.tagName)){wfApplyField(ev.target);wfDraw();}});
function wfApplyField(t){if(!wfEditable())return;const key=t.dataset.wfField,edge=WF.current.edges.find(e=>e.id===WF.selectedEdge),node=WF.current.nodes.find(n=>n.id===WF.selectedIds[0]);wfCheckpoint();if(key.startsWith('edge.')&&edge){const field=key.slice(5);if(field==='label')edge.label=t.value;else if(field==='targetSide'){edge.condition=edge.condition||{};edge.condition.targetSide=t.value;}else{edge.condition=edge.condition||{};if(field==='field'&&!t.value){delete edge.condition.field;delete edge.condition.operator;delete edge.condition.value;}else{edge.condition[field]=t.value;if(!edge.condition.operator)edge.condition.operator='equals';if(edge.condition.value===undefined)edge.condition.value='';}}}else if(node){if(key.startsWith('config.')){node.config=node.config||{};node.config[key.slice(7)]=t.value;}else{node[key]=t.value;if(key==='type'){WF.current.edges.filter(e=>e.source===node.id).forEach(e=>{if(!wfRegistry[node.type].outputs.includes(e.condition?.sourceHandle||'next')){e.condition=e.condition||{};e.condition.sourceHandle=wfRegistry[node.type].outputs[0]||'next';}});}if(key==='type'&&wfRegistry[t.value].responsible&&!node.config.responsibilityType){node.config.responsibilityType='ROLE';node.config.role='admin_staff';}}}wfChanged();wfUpdateLines();}
document.addEventListener('input',ev=>{if(UI.route!=='workflow')return;if(ev.target.id==='wfSearch'){WF.search=ev.target.value;const palette=document.querySelector('.wf-palette');const focus=ev.target.selectionStart;palette.innerHTML=wfPalette();const input=document.querySelector('#wfSearch');input.focus();input.setSelectionRange(focus,focus);}if(ev.target.matches('[data-wf-field]')&&['INPUT','TEXTAREA'].includes(ev.target.tagName)){wfApplyField(ev.target);if(['label','config.description'].includes(ev.target.dataset.wfField)){const n=WF.current.nodes.find(n=>n.id===WF.selectedIds[0]),body=n&&document.querySelector('[data-wfn="'+CSS.escape(n.id)+'"] .wf-node-body');if(body)body.innerHTML=wfNodeContent(n);}}});
document.addEventListener('dragstart',ev=>{const palette=ev.target.closest('[data-palette]');if(!palette||!wfEditable())return;ev.dataTransfer.setData('application/x-dts-node',palette.dataset.palette);ev.dataTransfer.effectAllowed='copy';});
document.addEventListener('dragover',ev=>{if(ev.target.closest('#wfBoard')&&wfEditable()&&Array.from(ev.dataTransfer.types).includes('application/x-dts-node')){ev.preventDefault();ev.dataTransfer.dropEffect='copy';}});
document.addEventListener('drop',ev=>{const board=ev.target.closest('#wfBoard');if(!board||!wfEditable())return;const paletteType=ev.dataTransfer.getData('application/x-dts-node');if(!wfRegistry[paletteType])return;const type=paletteType==='ACTION'?'REVIEW':paletteType;ev.preventDefault();const r=board.getBoundingClientRect(),x=(ev.clientX-r.left+board.scrollLeft)/WF.zoom,y=(ev.clientY-r.top+board.scrollTop)/WF.zoom;wfCheckpoint();const n={id:wfUid(),type,label:paletteType==='ACTION'?'Action':wfRegistry[type].label,x:wfSnap(x),y:wfSnap(y),config:wfRegistry[type].responsible?{responsibilityType:'ROLE',role:'admin_staff'}:{}};WF.current.nodes.push(n);WF.selectedIds=[n.id];wfChanged();wfDraw();});
const wfSnap=n=>Math.max(0,WF.snap?Math.round(n/16)*16:n);
let wfGesture=null;
function wfCanConnect(source,target,handle){const a=WF.current.nodes.find(n=>n.id===source),b=WF.current.nodes.find(n=>n.id===target);return !!(a&&b&&source!==target&&wfRegistry[a.type].outputs.includes(handle)&&wfRegistry[b.type].input&&!WF.current.edges.some(e=>e.source===source&&e.target===target&&((e.condition||{}).sourceHandle||'next')===handle));}
document.addEventListener('pointerdown',ev=>{const board=ev.target.closest('#wfBoard');if(!board)return;const output=ev.target.closest('[data-output]'),node=ev.target.closest('[data-wfn]');if(ev.button===1||WF.space||WF.hand){ev.preventDefault();wfGesture={kind:'pan',x:ev.clientX,y:ev.clientY,sx:board.scrollLeft,sy:board.scrollTop};return;}if(!wfEditable())return;if(output){ev.preventDefault();wfGesture={kind:'connect',source:output.dataset.output,handle:output.dataset.handle,moved:false};document.querySelectorAll('[data-input]').forEach(el=>el.classList.toggle('wf-valid-target',wfCanConnect(output.dataset.output,el.dataset.input,output.dataset.handle)));return;}if(node&&!ev.target.closest('.wf-handle')){const ids=WF.selectedIds.includes(node.dataset.wfn)?WF.selectedIds:[node.dataset.wfn];wfGesture={kind:'move',x:ev.clientX,y:ev.clientY,moved:false,before:wfGraphState(),nodes:WF.current.nodes.filter(n=>ids.includes(n.id)).map(n=>({n,x:n.x,y:n.y}))};}});
document.addEventListener('pointermove',ev=>{const g=wfGesture,board=document.querySelector('#wfBoard');if(!g||!board)return;if(g.kind==='pan'){board.scrollLeft=g.sx-(ev.clientX-g.x);board.scrollTop=g.sy-(ev.clientY-g.y);g.moved=true;return;}if(g.kind==='connect'){g.moved=true;const n=WF.current.nodes.find(n=>n.id===g.source),outs=wfRegistry[n.type].outputs,r=board.getBoundingClientRect(),x=(ev.clientX-r.left+board.scrollLeft)/WF.zoom,y=(ev.clientY-r.top+board.scrollTop)/WF.zoom,x1=n.x+190,y1=n.y+wfSize(n).height*(outs.indexOf(g.handle)+1)/(outs.length+1),m=(x1+x)/2,svg=document.querySelector('.wf-lines');svg.querySelector('.wf-connecting')?.remove();svg.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend','<path class="wf-connecting" d="M'+x1+' '+y1+' C'+m+' '+y1+' '+m+' '+y+' '+x+' '+y+'"/>');return;}if(Math.abs(ev.clientX-g.x)+Math.abs(ev.clientY-g.y)<4&&!g.moved)return;g.moved=true;g.nodes.forEach(o=>{o.n.x=wfSnap(o.x+(ev.clientX-g.x)/WF.zoom);o.n.y=wfSnap(o.y+(ev.clientY-g.y)/WF.zoom);const el=document.querySelector('[data-wfn="'+CSS.escape(o.n.id)+'"]');if(el){el.style.left=o.n.x+'px';el.style.top=o.n.y+'px';}});wfUpdateLines();});
document.addEventListener('pointerup',ev=>{const g=wfGesture;if(!g)return;if(g.kind==='connect'){const target=document.elementFromPoint(ev.clientX,ev.clientY)?.closest('[data-input]');if(target&&wfCanConnect(g.source,target.dataset.input,g.handle)){wfCheckpoint();WF.current.edges.push({id:wfUid(),source:g.source,target:target.dataset.input,label:g.handle==='next'?'':g.handle,condition:{sourceHandle:g.handle,targetSide:target.dataset.side==='right'?'right':'auto'}});wfChanged();}document.querySelectorAll('.wf-valid-target').forEach(el=>el.classList.remove('wf-valid-target'));wfDraw();}if(g.kind==='move'&&g.moved){WF.undo.push(g.before);WF.redo=[];wfChanged();}setTimeout(()=>{if(wfGesture===g)wfGesture=null;},0);});
document.addEventListener('pointercancel',()=>{wfGesture=null;document.querySelector('.wf-connecting')?.remove();});
document.addEventListener('wheel',ev=>{const board=ev.target.closest('#wfBoard');if(!board)return;ev.preventDefault();const r=board.getBoundingClientRect(),x=(ev.clientX-r.left+board.scrollLeft)/WF.zoom,y=(ev.clientY-r.top+board.scrollTop)/WF.zoom;WF.zoom=Math.max(.2,Math.min(2,WF.zoom*(ev.deltaY>0?.9:1.1)));const scene=document.querySelector('.wf-scene');scene.style.transform='scale('+WF.zoom+')';board.scrollLeft=x*WF.zoom-(ev.clientX-r.left);board.scrollTop=y*WF.zoom-(ev.clientY-r.top);document.querySelector('#wfZoomLabel').textContent=Math.round(WF.zoom*100)+'%';wfUpdateMini();},{passive:false});
document.addEventListener('scroll',ev=>{if(ev.target.id==='wfBoard')wfUpdateMini();},true);
document.addEventListener('keydown',ev=>{if(UI.route!=='workflow'||!WF.current||ev.target.closest('input,textarea,select,[contenteditable]'))return;const ctrl=ev.ctrlKey||ev.metaKey,k=ev.key.toLowerCase();if(k===' '){ev.preventDefault();WF.space=true;}if(k==='escape'){wfGesture=null;WF.selectedIds=[];WF.selectedEdge=null;wfDraw();}if(ctrl&&['s','z','y','d','c','v','a'].includes(k)){ev.preventDefault();if(k==='a'){WF.selectedIds=WF.current.nodes.map(n=>n.id);wfDraw();}else wfAction(({s:'save',z:ev.shiftKey?'redo':'undo',y:'redo',d:'duplicate',c:'copy',v:'paste'})[k]);}if(['delete','backspace'].includes(k)){ev.preventDefault();wfDelete();}if(k==='f'&&!ctrl)wfFit();});
document.addEventListener('keyup',ev=>{if(ev.key===' ')WF.space=false;});
window.addEventListener('beforeunload',ev=>{if(WF.dirty){ev.preventDefault();ev.returnValue='';}});
const baseWfRefresh=refresh;refresh=async function(...args){const ok=await baseWfRefresh(...args);if(ok&&UI.route==='workflow')await wfList();return ok;};


========================================
FILE: public/schedule.js
